# Conclusion fingerprint index

Auto-generated by `(fingerprint-index)` -- a PROTOTYPE retrieval view.  Each installed result is bucketed by the depth-3 *structural fingerprint* of its conclusion: head functor + immediate argument heads, recursively, with `·` for schema variables and numerals.  Both sides of a top `=`/`IFF` are kept, so the key is the redex skeleton the matcher fires on.  4538 results, 3418 buckets (2907 singletons); 2086 `-rev` companions folded into their base (tagged `(±)`).  Read-only.
